# International courier services — ambient air and road network

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This PCR is an authored candidate methodology. It covers the narrower service and route specified below; it does not claim complete CPC 68022 coverage. Quantities, time promise, supplier inventories and unresolved database identities require the explicit collection and qualification rules in this PCR.

## 2. Product Category Identity

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| canonical_pcr_id | pcr.distributive-trade-services-accommodation-food-and-beverage-serving-ser-18ff339a.postal-and-courier-services.international-courier-services |
| classification_refs | CPC 3.0 68022; narrower |
| covered_products | Non-USO international courier delivery of ambient, non-dangerous documents or parcels under one end-to-end responsibility contract; contracted road collection/delivery and air cargo linehaul; all physical handling outsourced. |
| excluded_products | USO letters/parcels (68011/68012); domestic courier 68021; stand-alone freight legs, brokerage, intermediation and counter-only services; sea/rail-only routes, owned transport/hubs, controlled-temperature or dangerous goods, oversized cargo; manufacture/use of the customer goods. |
| representative_product | One addressed parcel accepted at an origin in country A and delivered with admissible proof at a consignee address in country B; A differs from B. |
| production_route | Contracting courier controls acceptance, routing, tracking and delivery obligations; buys road freight, air freight, terminal handling, customs brokerage and storage as individually bounded services. Own administration is an all-electric office; equipment, occupancy, utilities and waste are inventoried. |
| market_state | Qualified completed courier delivery; no default delivery time, mass, size, country pair or return rate. |

## 3. Reference Flow

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| What | Complete international courier delivery, including collection, border crossing and final delivery under one responsible provider. |
| How much | 1 qualified delivered consignment; one consignment is one accepted waybill with a fixed piece list, not one package piece or one transport leg. |
| How well | Origin/destination countries, address gates, piece count, actual gross mass and dimensions, ambient condition, contents class, contract service promise and evidence of delivery/condition must be declared. Only complete contract-compliant deliveries are qualified. |
| How long or cycle | Acceptance cohort with all accepted waybills followed through final delivery, return, loss or disposal; no open/pending waybill in the final denominator. Contract duration and actual elapsed times are reported separately. |
| reference_flow_link | delivered_service |

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Reference amount | 1 |
| Reference product flow | Qualified international ambient courier delivery |
| Reference flow property | Number of items `01846770-4cfe-4a25-8ad9-919d8d378345` |
| Reference unit group | Units of items `5beb6eed-33a9-47b8-9ede-1dfe8f679159` |
| Reference unit | Item(s) |
| Required qualifiers | country pair; origin acceptance and destination delivery addresses; responsible provider and subcontractors; waybill/piece definition; gross mass and size class; contents and ambient state; air and road route segments; dates and service promise; proof/condition criteria; cohort closure and outcome counts; supplier inventory boundaries; allocation and electricity supply context |

## 4. Measurement and Unit Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Required property | Required unit | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| m_count | delivered_service | Number of items | Item(s) | Count Q qualified waybills, not piece scans, mass, invoices or transport jobs. Reference output Q/Q = 1; all burdens are cohort totals divided once by Q > 0. |
| m_transport | activity_origin_road; activity_air_linehaul; activity_destination_road | Goods transport (mass*distance) | t*km | For every actual segment use measured gross consignment cargo mass including packaging in kg divided by 1000, multiplied by actual segment km; sum loaded segments and allocate upstream empty movements explicitly. Do not convert chargeable/volumetric mass to physical mass or assume shortest routing. |
| m_storage | activity_bonded_storage | Mass*time | kg*d | Integrate actual stored cargo kg over each elapsed storage interval in days; entry/exit timestamps and changing stock determine kg*d. Neither shipment count nor a storage fee measures residence. |
| m_energy | office_electricity | Net calorific value | MJ | Metered electricity kWh becomes MJ by exactly 3.6 MJ/kWh; use energy property for this confirmed identity. Do not change it to a mass flow. |
| m_office | activity_office_occupancy | Area*time | m2*a | Measured occupied floor area multiplied by occupied calendar days divided by actual days in that year, then allocated once. No default floor area or service life. |
| m_mass | label_paper; protective_carton; office_water; office_wastewater; paper_waste; carton_waste; residual_waste; computer_hardware; computer_waste | Mass | kg | Keep each actual material or waste stream separate. Water volume converts using documented temperature-specific density; no unsupported volume-to-mass factor. Hardware is the physically measured net laptop mass amortized over observed service, not one laptop per delivery. |
| m_handling | activity_origin_handling; activity_destination_handling | Mass | kg | Handling kg matches the supplier intensity reference unit exactly: one complete terminal cycle per actual consignment passage and cargo kg, including all subtasks declared in that cycle. Repeated scans, screening, sorting, loading and unloading inside a complete cycle do not add another kg charge. A genuinely separate single-stage service requires its own bounded atomic row and matching stage intensity before completion; repeated complete passages may exceed unique cargo mass without adding reference deliveries. |
| m_customs | activity_customs_brokerage | Time | h | Use recorded brokerage staff hours for actual declaration, document correction and inspection coordination; 3600 seconds = 1 h; money, taxes and declaration counts cannot replace measured hours. |

## 5. System Boundary

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| b_end_to_end | all waybills | Start at recorded possession acceptance at the origin address; include pickup, every air and road connection, origin/destination/transit hubs, security inspection, applicable export/transit/import clearance, border storage, delivery attempts and final contractual delivery evidence. A border-crossing leg alone never meets the reference function. | `un-cpc3-68022`; `dhl-carriage-2024` |
| b_failure | cohort | Include actual rejected-after-acceptance, customs-held, damaged, returned, lost and disposed consignments and all redelivery/return legs in the same cohort numerator; exclude them from Q unless ultimately qualified. Rejection before custody is outside acceptance cohort; its office acceptance effort is allocated overhead, and any actual carrier custody/transport triggers accepted status. Pending cases block final qualification. | `dhl-carriage-2024` |
| b_services | all purchased services | Road and air services include their own fuel/energy supply, combustion species, vehicle/aircraft manufacture and maintenance, load-factor allocation and allocated empty repositioning. Handling includes screening, loading/unloading, terminal energy/equipment and terminal waste; storage includes its own space, utilities, equipment and waste. Supplier contracts and inventory must identify each inclusion. Do not add these burdens again as direct courier fuel, airport activity, assets or emissions. Missing provider inventory coverage is incomplete data, not zero. | `ghg-transport-2013` |
| b_partition | segments and subcontractors | Keep a unique service ledger keyed by waybill, segment/event, country/site, provider and time. Each physical activity has exactly one inventory owner; airport handling, brokerage and storage supporting physical processes cover only work outside transport/handling providers already included. A through-air service covering road feeder or terminal work requires removal of that overlap before use; a whole international courier service cannot also be a leg input. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| b_goods | contents and packaging | Customer goods and their original packaging enter custody as tracked cargo, not purchases of the courier; their manufacture and use are excluded but their actual gross mass affects transport. Courier-added corrugated cartons and paper labels are inventoried separately. Returned customer goods remain customer stock, not co-products; disposal requires measured material-specific waste and downstream treatment, never an invented replacement-goods credit. | `dhl-carriage-2024` |
| b_digital | activity_hosted_tracking | Include booking, customs-document exchange, tracking, dispatch and proof/claims data operation through measured hosted application activity; do not confuse server-instance h, staff h and shipment Item(s). Supplier networks/access coverage must end at a stated demarcation; any uncovered courier network equipment or telecom activity needs a separate atomic exchange before completion. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| b_office | own administration | Include office electricity, water, wastewater, paper, allocated laptop hardware, building occupancy, maintenance and end-of-life. Occupancy service includes building construction, fabric maintenance, HVAC plant and its refrigerant lifecycle, plus central HVAC, common lighting, cleaning and other building operations not covered by direct office cards. Exclude only the exact same utility quantity, period and physical scope already counted in the direct office meters/cards, together with separately counted laptops and direct office waste. Obtain whole-building/zone utility and cleaning/maintenance records by billing period; subtract reconciled directly assigned quantities for all beneficiary tenants/zones (not only this courier), allocate the residual common operational pool by occupied floor-area*time across all benefited tenants/zones, and retain the resulting utilities/materials/waste in the upstream occupancy inventory once. Missing central/shared operational records block completeness. No owned transport/hub, onsite fuel, dedicated refrigeration or generator is in this route; actual occurrence makes this route inapplicable unless the methodology is formally extended with separate atomic rows and protocols. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| b_unlisted | completeness | Apply the following concrete-row requirement to actual foreground components; an external service and compatible background evidence may cover its provider-internal components under foreground_interface_coverage without new foreground rows. At each site/provider reconcile actual purchasing, waste transfers, maintenance and services against the inventory. An unlisted actual material (including label adhesive/liner/ink), asset, cleaning chemical or disposed customer-content waste must receive a separate physically specific row, mass/energy/time/activity unit, linked collection protocol and the same Q denominator before completion; do not merge it into paper or residual waste by name. Service coverage evidence may locate it in an upstream supplier once, never waive it. | `ghg-product-2011` |

### Boundary Abstraction

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| declared_starting_condition | Customer-packed consignment accepted into courier custody at an origin address; no customer goods manufacture inside foreground. |
| starting_condition_role | service_acceptance_gate |
| product_classification_scope | CPC 3.0 68022 narrower: non-USO international ambient air/road courier service. |
| recursive_input_rule | Apply boundary_atomic_support: external activities enter through a defined single-unit service interface; do not recursively expand supplier internals. Foreground physical processes retain their existing cards and rules. Custody cargo is not a manufactured input. |
| upstream_dataset_requirement | Keep a signed scope-ownership matrix for each service contract and background dataset: exact gates, route/configuration, place/period, native reference unit, quality, supply/operation/empty/failure/maintenance/capital/terminal coverage, all-user allocation and foreground overlaps. Background coverage must preserve the existing lifecycle obligations and candidate allocation policies; it may be documented by a compatible dataset and supplier scope declaration without obtaining every internal exchange. Purchased quantity is measured at the interface; bundled independent services or incompatible units require separate concrete rows. For partial coverage partition by evidenced event/component first. Keep operator-owned assets, separately supplied energy/materials, customer-provided parts and operator-retained waste only in their own physical rows and exclude them from the supplier inventory. Suppress only identical covered supplier components; retain excluded real components through their own specific interface/physical rows. Never add the detailed counterpart to a service covering it; never erase burden because supplier detail is unavailable. Missing service-unit conversion, identity, coverage or a required complementary component blocks completeness, not zero. Existing narrower product applicability, physical stock balances, original-cohort corrections and receipt evidence remain mandatory for their actual owners. |
| disclosure | country pair; origin acceptance and destination delivery addresses; responsible provider and subcontractors; waybill/piece definition; gross mass and size class; contents and ambient state; air and road route segments; dates and service promise; proof/condition criteria; cohort closure and outcome counts; supplier inventory boundaries; allocation and electricity supply context |

### Atomic supporting-activity boundary

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| boundary_atomic_support | supporting activities | Each active exchange represents one defined material, equipment or service object with one primary property and unit. A service is not disallowed merely because it has upstream processes. Fix responsibility by provider, contract, site, event, gate and scope component before quantification. Operator-run processes and separately commissioned onsite tasks under operator inventory responsibility use foreground physical rows. Independently provided activities outside that responsibility use their specified service-interface rows; supplier manufacture, utilities, maintenance and waste remain in the matched background service, not extra foreground exchanges. A contract alone does not turn supplier internals into foreground. Do not change the product, route, custody gates or original reference denominator. Apply foreground_interface_method and foreground_interface_coverage; boundary changes need PCR review, not a data-availability choice. Ownership here means inventory responsibility, not legal title alone: rented equipment provision may be an external service while separately metered operator-run electricity remains foreground. |  |
| foreground_interface_coverage | all support processes and overlaps | Keep a signed scope-ownership matrix for each service contract and background dataset: exact gates, route/configuration, place/period, native reference unit, quality, supply/operation/empty/failure/maintenance/capital/terminal coverage, all-user allocation and foreground overlaps. Background coverage must preserve the existing lifecycle obligations and candidate allocation policies; it may be documented by a compatible dataset and supplier scope declaration without obtaining every internal exchange. Purchased quantity is measured at the interface; bundled independent services or incompatible units require separate concrete rows. For partial coverage partition by evidenced event/component first. Keep operator-owned assets, separately supplied energy/materials, customer-provided parts and operator-retained waste only in their own physical rows and exclude them from the supplier inventory. Suppress only identical covered supplier components; retain excluded real components through their own specific interface/physical rows. Never add the detailed counterpart to a service covering it; never erase burden because supplier detail is unavailable. Missing service-unit conversion, identity, coverage or a required complementary component blocks completeness, not zero. Existing narrower product applicability, physical stock balances, original-cohort corrections and receipt evidence remain mandatory for their actual owners. |  |

###### Paper substrate for shipment labels (`label_paper`)

Use only when actual occurrence is confirmed; documented absence may be not_applicable. Apply `inv_cohort_scope` in section 8.

- Selected flow: Paper substrate for shipment labels
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Actual paper substrate consumption = opening usable stock + receipts - closing usable stock - documented unused outbound transfers; allocate this physical consumption to the cohort then divide by Q. Reprints are included in the consumed stock, never added again; unused internal returns are already in closing stock and are not deducted twice. Adhesive, liner and ink remain separate supplemental exchanges when present.
- Value mode: Calculated from foreground (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)

###### Waste laptop computer hardware (`computer_waste`)

Use only when actual occurrence is confirmed; documented absence may be not_applicable. Apply `inv_cohort_scope` in section 8.

- Selected flow: Waste laptop computer hardware
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: M_EOL times cohort usage h divided by evidenced lifetime usage h and Q. M_EOL (kg) is observed retired residual laptop mass, or explicitly prospective residual end-of-life mass derived from measured current net mass with documented reuse/retention/removal plans, licensed receiver route and uncertainty. Still-used retained hardware is not an actual waste transfer now; its prospective lifecycle contribution is disclosed separately and revised on retirement. Never set M_EOL to zero only because no retirement has occurred; do not also add the actual transfer mass again to this normalized lifecycle contribution.
- Value mode: Calculated from foreground (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per declared reference flow
- Basis kind: Reference flow (`reference_flow`)
- Evidence kind: Calculated from collection (`calculated_from_collection`)
- Collection protocol: `cp_waste`
- Sources: `ghg-product-2011`

### Process: Support interface and foreground activity (Origin-country contracted ambient courier road carriage) (`support_origin_road`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Origin-country contracted ambient courier road carriage (`svc_origin_road`)

External interface only under boundary_atomic_support; one contract-defined origin-country contracted ambient courier road carriage. Measurement: sum(actual gross payload tonnes * actual loaded origin-road leg km), with original empty-leg and failure coverage. Fix provider, technology/configuration, gates, quality and included components per cp_interface_origin_road; use foreground_interface_coverage to exclude operator-owned or separately supplied components. No supplier-internal exchanges are additional foreground inputs. Allocation follows activity_origin_road for native activity attribution only, with foreground_interface_method; this does not require the physical inventory branch. UUID and property/unit-group support are unverified candidates. Calculation: calc_interface_origin_road.

- Selected flow: Origin-country contracted ambient courier road carriage
- Flow property / unit: Transport performance / t*km
- Amount rule: sum(A_s,e * alpha_s,e) / Q; native service unit t*km; normalize once per reference flow.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_interface_origin_road`

###### Diesel fuel supplied to the identified road vehicle (`u_origin_road_diesel`)

Foreground physical component only: apply foreground_interface_method and foreground_interface_coverage before this card; an external service covering this component uses its svc_ interface instead. Physical phase: use. Only if actual fuel identity and vehicle logs prove diesel operation; no default fuel assumption. A concrete candidate, not an assertion that it occurs. Apply `inv_origin_road_scope`, `inv_physical_basis` in section 8.

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| a_direct | all activities | Subdivide by actual provider, country pair, service class and route before allocating; retain document/parcel and mass/volume classes as separate strata, with Q and all associated failed activity for each stratum. Do not present strata with different function, promise or gates as equivalent. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| a_transport | road and air services | Use actual cargo t*km activity with a provider dataset matched to mode, vehicle/aircraft, fuel, region, utilization, loading constraint and empty movement coverage. Shared road multi-stop tours allocate trip burdens by per-segment cargo mass-distance; if volume is the binding constraint, use measured occupied volume-distance shares, document all cargo loads and test mass versus volume sensitivity. Allocate empty/repositioning to the serviced tour, not to zero cargo distance. For belly-hold and freighter legs use the primary mass method, raw/preallocated-data acceptance conditions and sensitivity in calc_air_payload_allocation. Supplier-method disclosure does not replace this algorithm, and a freighter dataset cannot silently substitute. | `ghg-transport-2013`; `ghg-product-2011` |
| a_hubs | handling storage customs | Allocate directly traced complete handling cycles to consignments; shared handling by actual gross mass per complete cycle only after validating equipment demand and supplier reference-unit scope. Do not subdivide a full-cycle intensity into repeated subtask kg. Storage uses measured kg*d and capacity constraint with volume-time sensitivity where relevant, brokerage recorded staff h. Keep routine sorting, extraordinary inspection and bonded detention distinguishable. Each shared pool uses complete beneficiary totals including failed and non-study work. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| a_office | office pools and assets | Assign dedicated records directly; shared office energy/water/occupancy/consumables use observed staff task-time share for the full acceptance cohort divided by all beneficiary task time, including pre-acceptance screening and failed cases. Hardware mass contribution equals measured laptop net mass multiplied by cohort usage h divided by evidenced total expected lifetime usage h; disclose replacement/repair records and sensitivity to remaining life. Laptop supply inventory ends before its separately inventoried end-of-life; exclude any supplier end-of-life burden already counted here. Use the full physical asset service lifetime through final retirement, including evidenced planned subsequent reuse, consistently for laptop net mass, lifetime_useh and M_EOL; retained/reused components are not current waste and have no substitution credit. No default lifetime, capacity or coefficient. Ordinary paper/carton/residual waste instead uses allocation_waste_origin with the task share of its same generation interval, preserving original-cohort responsibility. Static lifetime ratios describe the current version only; life/source changes and final retirement must apply calc_capital_restatement to the original cohorts, not update only future denominators. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| allocation_waste_origin | paper_waste; carton_waste; residual_waste | Fix each ordinary solid-waste generation event to its original service cohort and stratum at generation, never at later dispatch. Directly trace the generating task first; shared generation uses the existing site/handling allocation driver over the recorded generation interval, including all customer and noncohort workloads. Preserve event ID, generation date, original shares and original reference denominator through storage and internal transfers. Acquired old waste is not new generation. Retained waste keeps its original treatment responsibility; dispatch-period service does not inherit it. For shared generation, split records at each fixed local-calendar-month boundary (68013 uses its already declared counter accounting period instead); apply the existing site task/handling driver within that exact generation interval, not the full later dispatch/cohort window. Direct event attribution takes precedence. Record site timezone before period assignment. |  |
| a_waste | waste and returns | Do not allocate service burdens away to damaged/returned goods, waste or internal handoffs; no substitution credit is assumed. A waste output transfers the physical carrier to one named receiving route; attach downstream treatment once, and do not add its contained substances again as separate waste mass or treat CO2-equivalent as an elementary emission. | `ghg-product-2011` |

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| allocation_atomic_physical | supporting physical inventory | For each provider, site, configuration, event and accounting interval, partition every measured physical total into mutually exclusive inventory owners before selecting the study cohort. Use the fixed activity driver for that support process, with all users and failed work in the denominator. Direct causal attribution precedes shared allocation. Never multiply a cohort share a second time if already attributed. Keep service-level allocation quantities as raw drivers, not exchange quantities, and do not repeat division by the final reference count. Meter/stock, installed/replaced assets, internal transfers, generated waste and fate balances must reconcile at the same physical/time boundary. A complete product gate may retain its manufacturing background, but a lump service row may not be reintroduced under a different label. |  |
| allocation_atomic_capital | manufacture; installation; terminal assets | For each physical asset serial/cohort a, Q_a is its actual manufacturing/construction-gate quantity in the card unit. Set f_c,a=attributed physical lifetime activity_c,a/evidenced full-life all-user activity_a, using the process/asset-specific driver and identical configuration/capacity units. Manufacture q=sum(Q_a*f_c,a)/N_ref; each initial and whole-asset replacement creates its own asset cohort and enters once, not an additional current-period maintenance item. Installation physical quantities outside the asset product gate use that same installed-asset fraction. Terminal residual quantity in its own declared unit, evidenced from matched retirement or a prospective residual/reuse/retention plan, uses exactly the same f_c,a and original reference denominator; actual custody transfers only verify/update it. Earlier removed parts are excluded from terminal residual. Restate life and all original beneficiary cohorts under calc_capital_restatement; lifetime estimates need evidence and sensitivity, never numeric defaults. Genuine component repairs/replacements use actual causal events or the fixed provider maintenance-year shares, not another whole-life fraction. A whole component reused as a separately commissioned capital asset instead receives its own serial/lifetime register, never both treatments. |  |
| allocation_capital_restatement | computer_hardware; computer_waste; u_origin_road_road_vehicle_manufacture; u_origin_road_road_section_manufacture; u_origin_road_road_vehicle_terminal; u_origin_road_road_section_terminal; u_destination_road_road_vehicle_manufacture; u_destination_road_road_section_manufacture; u_destination_road_road_vehicle_terminal; u_destination_road_road_section_terminal; u_air_linehaul_aircraft_manufacture; u_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture; u_air_linehaul_aircraft_terminal; u_air_linehaul_runway_terminal; u_origin_handling_terminal_building_manufacture; u_origin_handling_conveyor_manufacture; u_origin_handling_screening_unit_manufacture; u_origin_handling_forklift_manufacture; u_origin_handling_terminal_building_terminal; u_origin_handling_conveyor_terminal; u_origin_handling_screening_unit_terminal; u_origin_handling_forklift_terminal; u_destination_handling_terminal_building_manufacture; u_destination_handling_conveyor_manufacture; u_destination_handling_screening_unit_manufacture; u_destination_handling_forklift_manufacture; u_destination_handling_terminal_building_terminal; u_destination_handling_conveyor_terminal; u_destination_handling_screening_unit_terminal; u_destination_handling_forklift_terminal; u_customs_brokerage_broker_computer_manufacture; u_customs_brokerage_broker_network_manufacture; u_customs_brokerage_broker_office_manufacture; u_customs_brokerage_broker_computer_terminal; u_customs_brokerage_broker_network_terminal; u_customs_brokerage_broker_office_terminal; u_bonded_storage_warehouse_manufacture; u_bonded_storage_storage_rack_manufacture; u_bonded_storage_storage_forklift_manufacture; u_bonded_storage_warehouse_terminal; u_bonded_storage_storage_rack_terminal; u_bonded_storage_storage_forklift_terminal; u_office_occupancy_office_building_manufacture; u_office_occupancy_hvac_plant_manufacture; u_office_occupancy_office_building_terminal; u_office_occupancy_hvac_plant_terminal; u_hosted_tracking_server_manufacture; u_hosted_tracking_storage_unit_manufacture; u_hosted_tracking_network_switch_manufacture; u_hosted_tracking_datacentre_manufacture; u_hosted_tracking_cooling_plant_manufacture; u_hosted_tracking_server_terminal; u_hosted_tracking_storage_unit_terminal; u_hosted_tracking_network_switch_terminal; u_hosted_tracking_datacentre_terminal; u_hosted_tracking_cooling_plant_terminal | This PCR uses full restatement, not frozen historical shares or a retirement-period catch-up. All direct and supporting capital, reusable containers, installation outside the product gate and paired terminal sources follow calc_capital_restatement. Each specific activity rule fixes the driver and beneficiary shares; these are applied once. A complete asset background already including delivery/end-of-life follows the same restatement, without separate duplicate terminal exchanges. Routine component maintenance remains under its causal/period method. |  |
| allocation_atomic_waste | operational and maintenance wastes | Generated operational and maintenance waste retains its original generation event, provider/activity cohort, shares and original reference denominator through storage and transfers. Fixed shared-waste intervals are local calendar months with timezone declared; use same-interval causal activity and all users, unless an existing more specific maintenance-year or counter-period method applies. Reconcile closing=opening+generation+internal_in-internal_out-external_dispatch-evidenced_physical_loss on consistent material/moisture bases. Include supported eventual storage/transport/treatment for retained waste, with actual evidence or an explicitly evidenced compatible prospective route and uncertainty; unknown fate remains incomplete. Later receipts replace that original event model, not add a new dispatch-period burden. Asset terminal-waste uses its paired capital fraction instead. Waste carrier and its constituents, storage losses/releases and residual treatment cannot be counted twice; the same measured site/storage operation remains under one inventory owner. |  |

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| allocation_provider_maintenance_period | u_origin_road_removed_tyre; u_origin_road_lubricant; u_origin_road_used_lubricant; u_destination_road_removed_tyre; u_destination_road_lubricant; u_destination_road_used_lubricant; u_air_linehaul_engine_oil; u_air_linehaul_used_engine_oil; u_origin_road_replacement_tyre; u_destination_road_replacement_tyre | For every shared routine supporting-provider maintenance pool, fix interval y to the site-local calendar year with timezone and exact asset/site/configuration recorded. Split a cohort crossing years into its within-year activity. Actual installed/consumed material M_x,y and its separately measured generated waste use s_c,y=cohort-attributed beneficiary driver_y/all-beneficiary driver_y for that same year, then q=sum_y(M_x,y*s_c,y)/N_ref once. The driver is the physical support-process activity fixed in activity_*; for vehicle upkeep it is actual vehicle kilometres including once-attributed empty/failure travel, for variable road upkeep it is evidenced class-specific engineering damage in the same year, and for building fabric it is reserved floor-area-calendar-hours. Other shared support equipment uses its fixed actual all-beneficiary task/cycle/union-hour driver; retain exact configuration strata and no concurrency duplication. A signed maintenance work order fixes the causal asset and task pool before calculation, not a later modeller choice. Specific accident/failure repair is allocated exclusively to its evidenced causal order/task set, never also to the annual routine pool. Genuine component consumption excludes unused opening/closing stocks, uninstalled returns and internal transfers; generated removed parts retain this original event/year/share through later custody. No assumption that input and removed-part masses are identical. Apply one annual period share, not whole-life f, to real component repairs; entire replacement assets create separate capital cohorts instead. A positive unsupported pool or zero/unknown all-user denominator blocks completeness. Apply the same rules to retained provider maintenance cards where previously referenced; independent specific generation/fate balances and exact overlap exclusions remain mandatory. |  |

Independent origin road segment operation; actual contracted vehicle, energy, direct emissions, capital, maintenance, empty positioning and end-of-life, excluding air/terminal modules already owned elsewhere. Retain the service activity only in raw records and allocation; do not create a replacement activity exchange. For each physical supplier row j and matched period p, q_j = sum(X_j,p * alpha_cohort,p) / final_denominator, with calibration and all beneficiaries in alpha. If source activity is already per reference flow, first recover its original raw total and denominator; apply the matched per-activity physical intensity, attribution and original final denominator only once. All real losses, failed tasks and empty/standby capacity stay in their fixed pools. Capital and terminal residuals use the same f; no default asset mass/life or intensity. Retain signed supplier scope and exact overlap crosswalk to existing direct rows. Obtain chemistry/BOM/species identity before adding a missing concrete row; an unbound candidate can await official identity but cannot be a plural collection. Measure all events, reconcile per local month/maintenance year as prescribed and review the complete asset/supplier inventory annually and at configuration/retirement changes. Unmeasured positive activity, unsupported future treatment or an unlisted actual exchange prevents completeness. Apply allocation_provider_maintenance_period and calc_provider_maintenance_period to this provider actual routine/causal maintenance; no whole-cohort distance in each year. Use calc_contracted_road_ac for traction. Never apply the battery formula to stationary premises AC; assign that AC from its separate measured ledger under its physical driver. Measure non-traction in its own ledger under calc_contracted_road_ac, then use its q_card or the distinct supply row as applicable. | card-native units; raw t*km | every event; local-month/maintenance-year reconciliation; annual/asset-change review | full original acceptance cohort and failure/return tail, matched asset life and eventual waste evidence | same provider/site/configuration scope; all shared users | per reference flow | calibrated originals; signed scope and overlap map; asset/receipt lineage; uncertainty |

### Calculation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| calc_digital | activity_hosted_tracking | q_hosted = sum(instance elapsed seconds/3600 * measured cohort resource-time/all beneficiary resource-time)/Q; match each configuration to its supplier inventory and include allocated standby once. These are raw provider activity diagnostics only; they are not inventory exchanges. Distribute only the separately named physical rows in the corresponding support processes, using original raw activity and one final reference normalization. | cp_digital; cp_cohort | raw activity diagnostic: configured application h per reference consignment | `ghg-product-2011` |
| foreground_interface_method | svc_origin_road; svc_destination_road; svc_air_linehaul; svc_origin_handling; svc_destination_handling; svc_customs_brokerage; svc_bonded_storage; svc_office_occupancy; svc_hosted_tracking | Before measurement classify every event/scope component as foreground physical or external service under boundary_atomic_support, with no overlap. Apply existing physical-card formulas, component rules and cp_support protocols only to the former, never demand an external provider BOM or internal meter ledger to activate a service interface. For a service row s collect raw A_s,e in its fixed native unit and alpha_s,e from the existing named activity allocation: direct cohort events have alpha=1; genuinely shared events retain the original all-user driver, failure/empty/standby treatment and event window. Allocated service amount T_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e); if the native activity ledger already contains cohort-attributed activity, alpha is already applied and must not be multiplied again. q_s=T_s/N_ref, with N_ref the original positive cohort reference output. Preserve raw totals for zero output and mark the unit result incomplete. Do not convert time, area, count or transport activity to energy or equipment mass without a separate evidenced relationship. Do not apply physical capital life fractions again to a service inventory that already contains its capital allocation. Quantities for background linkage equal q_s times an inventory per one identical service unit, once; an emission factor is not the service exchange quantity. All old physical-only instructions and service prohibitions apply only to the foreground physical branch; the service branch follows this rule and its explicit interface protocol. Incomplete or aborted service events retain their evidenced attributable burden. Do not silently discard them because the interface counts completed units: document an equivalent native service quantity supported by the contract and execution records, or add a separately identified partial-service row before claiming completeness; never assume a full unit or zero. A supplier value already per the final reference is not a raw A_s,e: recover its original total and denominator before recomputation, or prove exact equivalent normalization without dividing by the final denominator again. Any included supplier allocation is applied only once. | cp_interface_origin_road; cp_interface_destination_road; cp_interface_air_linehaul; cp_interface_origin_handling; cp_interface_destination_handling; cp_interface_customs_brokerage; cp_interface_bonded_storage; cp_interface_office_occupancy; cp_interface_hosted_tracking | native service units per original reference flow |  |
| calc_interface_origin_road | svc_origin_road | Apply foreground_interface_method. Native activity A_s,e (t*km): sum(actual gross payload tonnes * actual loaded origin-road leg km), with original empty-leg and failure coverage. Use the existing activity_origin_road beneficiary and event-window rules only for service activity attribution; the physical branch is not required. q_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e)/Q once. A compatible supplier intensity must use exactly this unit, scope and original allocation policy; no standard activity per final output is assumed. | cp_interface_origin_road; activity_origin_road | t*km/Q |  |
| calc_interface_destination_road | svc_destination_road | Apply foreground_interface_method. Native activity A_s,e (t*km): sum(actual gross payload tonnes * actual loaded destination-road leg km), with original empty-leg and failure coverage. Use the existing activity_destination_road beneficiary and event-window rules only for service activity attribution; the physical branch is not required. q_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e)/Q once. A compatible supplier intensity must use exactly this unit, scope and original allocation policy; no standard activity per final output is assumed. | cp_interface_destination_road; activity_destination_road | t*km/Q |  |
| calc_interface_air_linehaul | svc_air_linehaul | Apply foreground_interface_method. Native activity A_s,e (t*km): sum(actual gross freight tonnes * actual flown loaded-leg km); the background service must reproduce calc_air_payload_allocation, including causal empty flights; do not apply its passenger/freight share again to purchased t*km. Use the existing activity_air_linehaul beneficiary and event-window rules only for service activity attribution; the physical branch is not required. q_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e)/Q once. A compatible supplier intensity must use exactly this unit, scope and original allocation policy; no standard activity per final output is assumed. | cp_interface_air_linehaul; activity_air_linehaul | t*km/Q |  |
| calc_interface_origin_handling | svc_origin_handling | Apply foreground_interface_method. Native activity A_s,e (kg): sum(actual gross freight kg for each distinct complete terminal passage); repeated actual passages retain distinct IDs, no universal kg-per-parcel. Use the existing activity_origin_handling beneficiary and event-window rules only for service activity attribution; the physical branch is not required. q_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e)/Q once. A compatible supplier intensity must use exactly this unit, scope and original allocation policy; no standard activity per final output is assumed. | cp_interface_origin_handling; activity_origin_handling | kg/Q |  |
| calc_interface_destination_handling | svc_destination_handling | Apply foreground_interface_method. Native activity A_s,e (kg): sum(actual gross freight kg for each distinct complete terminal passage); repeated actual passages retain distinct IDs, no universal kg-per-parcel. Use the existing activity_destination_handling beneficiary and event-window rules only for service activity attribution; the physical branch is not required. q_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e)/Q once. A compatible supplier intensity must use exactly this unit, scope and original allocation policy; no standard activity per final output is assumed. | cp_interface_destination_handling; activity_destination_handling | kg/Q |  |
| calc_interface_customs_brokerage | svc_customs_brokerage | Apply foreground_interface_method. Native activity A_s,e (h): actual case-linked agent work hours, including failed/reworked work; concurrent work is partitioned using the retained all-client rule. Use the existing activity_customs_brokerage beneficiary and event-window rules only for service activity attribution; the physical branch is not required. q_s=sum(A_s,e*alpha_s,e)/Q once. Internal transfers preserve origin and do not create another exchange. Model the original cohort waste output and its required storage/transport/final treatment even while physically retained, using actual compatible receiver evidence or an explicitly evidenced material/composition/location-matched prospective route with uncertainty. Include every necessary lifecycle leg once through a scope crosswalk; exclude site/storage energy and handling already included by site/service inventories. The generated-waste exchange is at the generation gate. Its linked complete waste-management inventory takes one kg on that same generated-mass basis through storage and all subsequent fates; final-treatment input is only the compatible measured/evidenced forecast residual after storage losses/moisture conversion, not the full original kg plus separate losses. The retained asset count is multiplied by f, not replaced by hours. Missing actual asset/task/lifetime mapping blocks completion. Original terminal residual uses exactly this f; actual component replacement events use their matched maintenance-period causal attribution; whole-asset replacements form new independently allocated asset cohorts. Apply this denominator separately to every actual initial/whole-replacement asset serial/cohort; do not pool a successor into the retired asset or assign its full quantity by current-period maintenance share. | calc_atomic_species | all elementary physical rows | Foreground physical branch only under foreground_interface_method; service quantities use their interface rule. Each elementary row identifies one substance or physically specified particle carrier and one receiving medium/subcompartment. Collect the actual substance/CAS or verified chemical identity, factor unit and mass/molar/carbon expression, source technology/fuel/load, location and receiving medium. Apply a substance-specific measured release or compatible factor once. Factory emissions already in a complete product gate are not also direct provider-use emissions. Particle size classes and fate fractions are mutually exclusive; physically defined particles are not fictitious pure elements. NOx and NMVOC totals are QA observations only, never elementary-exchange substitutes. Every actual extra species/material needs its own concrete bilingual source row, protocol, unit, fixed calculation and normal projection before completed modelling; do not use a selector or a family-name placeholder. | cp_support_origin_road; cp_support_destination_road; cp_support_air_linehaul; cp_support_origin_handling; cp_support_destination_handling; cp_support_customs_brokerage; cp_support_bonded_storage; cp_support_office_occupancy; cp_support_hosted_tracking | one substance or defined physical carrier per receiver |  |
| calc_nox_speciation | u_origin_road_no_air; u_origin_road_no2_air; u_destination_road_no_air; u_destination_road_no2_air; u_air_linehaul_no_air; u_air_linehaul_no2_air | For the explicitly evidenced definition NOx=NO+NO2 expressed as NO2-equivalent, record T, its unit/basis and source-matched measured molar fraction a=n_NO/(n_NO+n_NO2). Then m_NO=a*T*M_NO/M_NO2 and m_NO2=(1-a)*T, using independently evidenced molecular masses on the same unit basis. Prefer direct substance-specific mass measurements/factors when available and do not add the QA total again. T is not ordinary NO/NO2 mixture mass; never apply a mass fraction directly to T or assign all T to NO2. If definition includes other nitrogen species, speciation or conversion evidence is missing, no two-component split is permitted: preserve the raw QA observation and block completion until actual species-specific rows and quantities are established. No numeric fraction or molecular mass is defaulted by this PCR. | cp_support_origin_road; cp_support_destination_road; cp_support_air_linehaul | kg NO and kg NO2, never NO2-equivalent exchange |  |

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| calc_provider_maintenance_period | u_origin_road_removed_tyre; u_origin_road_lubricant; u_origin_road_used_lubricant; u_destination_road_removed_tyre; u_destination_road_lubricant; u_destination_road_used_lubricant; u_air_linehaul_engine_oil; u_air_linehaul_used_engine_oil; u_origin_road_replacement_tyre; u_destination_road_replacement_tyre | For every shared routine supporting-provider maintenance pool, fix interval y to the site-local calendar year with timezone and exact asset/site/configuration recorded. Split a cohort crossing years into its within-year activity. Actual installed/consumed material M_x,y and its separately measured generated waste use s_c,y=cohort-attributed beneficiary driver_y/all-beneficiary driver_y for that same year, then q=sum_y(M_x,y*s_c,y)/N_ref once. The driver is the physical support-process activity fixed in activity_*; for vehicle upkeep it is actual vehicle kilometres including once-attributed empty/failure travel, for variable road upkeep it is evidenced class-specific engineering damage in the same year, and for building fabric it is reserved floor-area-calendar-hours. Other shared support equipment uses its fixed actual all-beneficiary task/cycle/union-hour driver; retain exact configuration strata and no concurrency duplication. A signed maintenance work order fixes the causal asset and task pool before calculation, not a later modeller choice. Specific accident/failure repair is allocated exclusively to its evidenced causal order/task set, never also to the annual routine pool. Genuine component consumption excludes unused opening/closing stocks, uninstalled returns and internal transfers; generated removed parts retain this original event/year/share through later custody. No assumption that input and removed-part masses are identical. Apply one annual period share, not whole-life f, to real component repairs; entire replacement assets create separate capital cohorts instead. A positive unsupported pool or zero/unknown all-user denominator blocks completeness. Apply the same rules to retained provider maintenance cards where previously referenced; independent specific generation/fate balances and exact overlap exclusions remain mandatory. | cp_support_origin_road; cp_support_destination_road; cp_support_air_linehaul | native physical unit per original reference flow |  |

| capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture_occupancy_basis | u_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture; u_air_linehaul_runway_terminal | Foreground physical branch only under foreground_interface_method; service quantities use their interface rule. Component of capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture. Before execution, read the complete group listed by the applicable entry: its definitions, conditions and exclusions govern every component. Use only that entry's actual event scope and original denominator; this component is not a separate exchange or independently additive result. Register actual handover area Q_a (m2) for each uniquely identified runway asset a and configuration interval, and its complete-life movements for all beneficiaries or evidenced same-scope historic/forecast traffic. For each movement e, airport evidence identifies the effective runway/section area A_e and actual occupancy time t_e, giving V_e=A_e*t_e; partition concurrent section use into measured nonoverlapping occupancy rather than copying the whole section. Do not substitute flight hours, cargo kg or flight counts for occupancy. | cp_support_air_linehaul; cp_transport; a_transport; cp_cohort | Method component; the originating entry owns the inventory result |  |
| capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture_movement_attribution | u_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture; u_air_linehaul_runway_terminal | Foreground physical branch only under foreground_interface_method; service quantities use their interface rule. Component of capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture. Before execution, read the complete group listed by the applicable entry: its definitions, conditions and exclusions govern every component. Use only that entry's actual event scope and original denominator; this component is not a separate exchange or independently additive result. For an occupied revenue movement let w_e = p_freight,e * s_cohort,e, using the evidenced passenger/freight split under a_transport and the cohort share of all freight beneficiaries on the same leg. Use p_freight,e=1 for full-freight movements and w_e=0 for movements without an attributable cohort share; include passenger, freight, training and empty/reposition movements in the all-user denominator. For each empty/repositioning movement use dispatch evidence to join all causal operating legs t. For a single causal leg use w_e=w_t. For several use w_e=sum_t(H_t*w_t)/sum_all_causal(H_t), with H_t the evidenced block hours of each unique causal loaded leg, counted once across waybills. Use the occupied-leg passenger/freight and all-freight beneficiary shares above for each w_t. Passenger-only or evidenced unrelated legs remain in this denominator with w_t=0. Apply the same rule to all beneficiaries. An incomplete causal register, missing matched block hours or a nonpositive denominator leaves allocation unresolved; keep known occupancy in the all-life area-time denominator. Use w_e=0 for independent nonfreight training with evidenced no cohort relation. | cp_support_air_linehaul; cp_transport; a_transport; cp_cohort | Method component; the originating entry owns the inventory result |  |
| capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture_lifetime_quantities | u_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture; u_air_linehaul_runway_terminal | Foreground physical branch only under foreground_interface_method; service quantities use their interface rule. Component of capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture. Before execution, read the complete group listed by the applicable entry: its definitions, conditions and exclusions govern every component. Use only that entry's actual event scope and original denominator; this component is not a separate exchange or independently additive result. f_c,a=sum(V_e*w_e)/sum_all_life(V_e); denominator uses all users, the same life boundary and unit. Actual use bears idle construction capacity; do not drop it. Do not multiply the passenger/freight share again. q_build=sum(Q_a*f_c,a)/Q and q_EOL=sum(R_net,a*f_c,a)/Q, using terminal net kg without repeating historical deductions. Preserve the same asset, configuration and evidenced life; an allocated intensity must reproduce this closure, otherwise missing runway/weight evidence blocks completion. Remove exact construction, upkeep and terminal overlap with flight/handling inventories. Attribute maintenance as actual events, not a second lifetime fraction. | cp_support_air_linehaul; cp_transport; a_transport; cp_cohort | Method component; the originating entry owns the inventory result |  |
| capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture_linked_method_controls | u_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture; u_air_linehaul_runway_terminal | Foreground physical branch only under foreground_interface_method; service quantities use their interface rule. Component of capital_air_linehaul_runway_manufacture. Before execution, read the complete group listed by the applicable entry: its definitions, conditions and exclusions govern every component. Use only that entry's actual event scope and original denominator; this component is not a separate exchange or independently additive result. Static lifetime ratios describe the current version only; life/source changes and final retirement must apply calc_capital_restatement to the original cohorts, not update only future denominators. Passenger/freight and within-leg freight shares must come from calc_air_payload_allocation; its primary mass method, zero-freight/empty branches and preallocated-data acceptance rules govern this row without another allocation choice. | cp_support_air_linehaul; cp_transport; a_transport; cp_cohort | Method component; the originating entry owns the inventory result |  |


### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| dq_occurrence | all conditional cards | Confirm occurrence from dated purchase/operating/transfer and no-event records at every site. Mark not_applicable only when actual absence is demonstrated and signed; an absent UUID, invoice or missing meter never proves zero. Unknown occurrence or amount remains incomplete and blocks final conformance. | signed applicability and records coverage matrix |
| quality_waste_origin | paper_waste; carton_waste; residual_waste | For every ordinary solid-waste stream verify generation responsibility, original cohort/stratum/denominator, origin-labelled stock balance, supported actual or prospective complete treatment and later-receipt replacement. Unknown origin/fate/physical loss remains an explicit gap; future dispatch alone cannot close or reassign it. Keep wastewater and amortized terminal-asset treatment on their separate methods. | generation/stock/transfer ledgers; supported route models; original-cohort receipt replacements |
| dq_identity | all inventory and reference identities | Blank UUIDs are explicit semantic candidates, not validated bindings. Before database-ready use, verify exact official flow, type, state, property/unit and waste receiving route from direct official responses with hash-bound receipts; compare exact official Chinese names. Historical electricity identity observation does not establish current supply technology, current database status or methodological approval. | direct-response identity receipts; support-record chain; unresolved list |
| dq_supplier | all upstream services | Obtain physical service units and primary inventory coverage, not spend-only or CO2e-only figures. Match each mode/leg, handling, storage and building service to its recorded provider boundary and explicit missing categories. Complete LCA use needs all relevant elementary species and receiving media in upstream inventories; a GHG transport guide alone cannot establish that. | supplier inventory/boundary matrix; actual activity; gap register |
| dq_uncertainty | measurements and conversion | Retain raw values, unit, timestamp, calibration, source document and responsible collector; reconcile to invoices, meters, task times and transfers. Report measured/estimated/missing separately, method and uncertainty; test dominant load, passenger/freight allocation, volume constraint and lifetime assumptions. No invented default factors, zero fill, generic ranges or compliance limits. | calibration/estimation records; reconciliation; sensitivity results |
| dq_air_nonco2 | activity_air_linehaul | Report climate characterization method and whether aviation non-CO2 effects (including contrails and altitude effects) are covered, excluded or uncertain; disclose this limitation separately from physical fuel/emission inventory. Do not multiply CO2 or CO2e by a default uplift or claim comprehensive aviation climate coverage from a cargo activity or GHG-only factor. | method version; aviation scope disclosure; sensitivity where supported |
| dq_bilingual | both language renderings and projection | Audit every card identity/direction/type/unit/formula/condition, qualifier and normative requirement in both languages; retain the same rule IDs, row IDs, protocol ownership and source links. Regenerate structured from English and compare actual projected meaning; a fingerprint alone is insufficient. | bilingual semantic review; projection readback |

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| quality_atomic_completeness | all disaggregated support and species | Completeness is branch-specific under foreground_interface_method and foreground_interface_coverage. Foreground physical exchanges need the retained exact identities, original measurements, species and stock reconciliation. External interfaces need one defined service/property/unit, measured activity, exact supplier gates and compatible background coverage, not exhaustive provider-internal BOMs or capital ledgers. Unknown occurrence is not absence; uncovered real scope is an explicit gap. Unbound interface UUID/property/unit-group identities remain row-specific candidates until direct official evidence and receipts resolve them. The interface is not permission for an undefined multi-service package or for an Item(s) substitute for hours/mass/transport activity. | scope-to-physical-row reconciliation; supplier/BOM/species originals; signed gap register |

## 9. Validation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| v_scope | dataset | Verify non-USO custody at an origin and completed delivery in a different country under one contracting provider; all actual routes match the declared ambient outsourced air/road/office scope. Domestic-only, USO and leg-only data fail this PCR. | `un-cpc3-68022` |
| validate_service_interfaces | svc_origin_road; svc_destination_road; svc_air_linehaul; svc_origin_handling; svc_destination_handling; svc_customs_brokerage; svc_bonded_storage; svc_office_occupancy; svc_hosted_tracking | Verify the event/component responsibility matrix before reading row amounts. An external-service component activates its interface only; its covered detailed physical counterpart is inactive, not a measured zero. Verify one property/unit, raw native activity, original all-user allocation and single final normalization; failed/reworked/empty events follow the retained cohort rules. Test partial contracts, own energy/assets/retained wastes, already allocated supplier inventories, zero-output cohorts and missing complementary coverage. Reject double counting, invented identity/amounts, forced supplier-internal collection or an undocumented branch change. Verify all service row IDs and protocol/rule links in both languages and generated guidance. |  |
| v_cohort | delivered_service | Require Q > 0, exclusive final outcome closure, pending = 0, consistent fixed piece lists and all failures/returns/reattempts in numerator; count Q once. Incomplete customs holds cannot be deleted or declared zero. | `dhl-carriage-2024` |
| v_inventory | inventory | Require unique activity ownership and supplier coverage, complete physical units/calculations, matched collection records, documented allocation and all actual atomic exchanges. Reject double-counted transport energy/assets/terminal work, unowned waste treatment, unresolved numerical conversions or unexplained missing quantities. Test A-period generated retained waste and B-period dispatch: original A responsibility and denominator persist, and actual evidence replaces that same event model without another B burden. | `ghg-product-2011` |
| v_identity | database-ready implementation | An authored candidate with unresolved identities is not publish-ready. Resolve every applicable flow/property/unit identity against official evidence, retain excluded/not-applicable decisions, and report remaining gaps without claiming verified database completeness or scientific approval. |  |
| v_language | source and guidance | Require aligned bilingual reference basis, boundaries, all applicable inventory cards, all collection/calculation/quality rules, row/protocol links and source coverage in actual generated guidance; preserve candidate lifecycle until independent review. |  |
| validate_air_payload_allocation | all mixed/freighter/empty legs | Verify C/P/M_c, same-leg basis, all beneficiaries, load-estimation evidence and zero-freight/empty causal branches under calc_air_payload_allocation. Recompute primary w and preallocated-data deduplication. Unsupported passenger mass, a different primary algorithm or repeated p_freight blocks completion. |  |

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| validate_atomic_support | all support and emission rows | Verify the event/component responsibility matrix before reading row amounts. An external-service component activates its interface only; its covered detailed physical counterpart is inactive, not a measured zero. Verify one property/unit, raw native activity, original all-user allocation and single final normalization; failed/reworked/empty events follow the retained cohort rules. Test partial contracts, own energy/assets/retained wastes, already allocated supplier inventories, zero-output cohorts and missing complementary coverage. Reject double counting, invented identity/amounts, forced supplier-internal collection or an undocumented branch change. Verify all service row IDs and protocol/rule links in both languages and generated guidance. |  |

## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | unit_process |
| downstream_use | secondary_dataset; background_dataset |
| allowed_use | Quantified international ambient courier delivery within the declared contracted air/road network, country pair and service stratum after all applicability and data gates pass. |
| excluded_use | Not a universal transport emission factor; no USO/domestic/counter/stand-alone freight substitution; not whole product life-cycle impact or a verified current Tiangong dataset while gaps remain. |
| required_metadata | country pair; origin acceptance and destination delivery addresses; responsible provider and subcontractors; waybill/piece definition; gross mass and size class; contents and ambient state; air and road route segments; dates and service promise; proof/condition criteria; cohort closure and outcome counts; supplier inventory boundaries; allocation and electricity supply context; ordinary-waste generation origin, original shares/denominator, retained stocks and treatment revision chain; signed foreground/interface responsibility matrix, native service activity and background coverage; original physical activity, manufacture/terminal pairing and species basis only for foreground-owned components |
| required_quality_disclosure | Q, A, R0 and failures; pending; cohort period and elapsed closure; supplier activity coverage; identities pending; estimates/calibration; all allocation bases and sensitivity; exclusions and unresolved actual exchanges. |
| update_trigger | Provider, mode, country pair, promise, package class, office technology, supplier inventory, quantity basis, allocation, legal customs process or identity evidence changes.; later ordinary-waste receipts/fate or generation correction update the original event/cohort model; foreground physical identity/speciation changes or external interface configuration/unit/coverage changes require applicable concrete-row review and fresh projection |

## 11. Data Sources

| Source id | Type | Reference | Used for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| un-cpc3-68022 | official_guidance | UNSD, CPC Version 3.0, 68022 International courier services; https://unstats.un.org/unsd/classifications/Econ/Structure/Detail/EN/2100/68022 | Non-USO collection, transport and delivery to international destinations; source defines a wider multi-mode category than this PCR. |
| dhl-carriage-2024 | standard | DHL Express, Terms and Conditions of Carriage (2024), sections 1–5 and 11; https://mydhlplus.dhl.com/content/dam/downloads/global/en/t-c/terms_conditions_of_carriage_en-2024.pdf.coredownload.pdf | Operational case evidence for customs, inspection, delivery, rejected/returned shipments, waybill identification and actual versus chargeable weight; not a universal legal rule or numeric LCA factor. |
| ghg-product-2011 | standard | GHG Protocol, Product Life Cycle Accounting and Reporting Standard (2011), chapter 9; https://ghgprotocol.org/sites/default/files/standards/Product-Life-Cycle-Accounting-Reporting-Standard_041613.pdf | Subdivision before physical allocation; general GHG accounting principles inform this PCR without establishing multi-impact compliance. |
| ghg-transport-2013 | official_guidance | GHG Protocol, Technical Guidance for Calculating Scope 3 Emissions, version 1.0 (2013), category 4, pp. 53–55; https://ghgprotocol.org/sites/default/files/standards/Scope3_Calculation_Guidance_0.pdf | Transport physical activity and mass/volume allocation; no default load, empty return, air factor or volumetric conversion adopted. |
